*But they've been scrubbed from my mind so thoroughly that I cannot begin to guess at what they may have been.*
*All that remains is an incredible sense of unease.*

Sophitia: "..."

Kakeru: "Is that any way to talk to someone you're asking for help?"

*I guess if we leave the girl to Sophie she'll eventually find out her name and address at least.*
*Once we know that, our opponents won't have their veil of mystery to hide behind.*

*If Sora and Kujo manage to get Kosaka-senpai to tell them Rig Veda's plans, that'll be even better.*
*Even if one of them turns out to be the User of the Evil Eye and they plan on killing more people, as long as we know their plans in detail, we can stop them.*
*We're still in the information-gathering phase before the final showdown.*
*However, this is the most critical juncture.*
*If we can win ourselves an early advantage...*
*...*
*I just realized something awful.*
Kakeru: "I still haven't done a damn thing, have I?"
*It was a total fluke that I found out Takamine's first name. That was really Yoichi, not me.*
*And when you look beyond that, zilch. Man, I need to work harder.*
*I get up and change out of my uniform.*
*I hope we don't end up in another crisis, but if we do, that'll be my time to shine.*
*If nothing else, I can act as a shield for Sora, Kujo, and Yuuki.*

Kakeru: "...Yeah, probably."
Sora: "Maybe I should just never heal!"
Kakeru: "Hey, don't say that, not even as a joke."
Sora: "For real though, even if everyone else forgets me, I'm fine as long as you still remember me, onii-chan."
*Smiling, Sora starts swinging her hand back and forth.*
*That... didn't sound like a joke to me.*
*Well. If all she wants is to maintain the status quo, that's probably not enough to worsen her symptoms.*
*While that's probably not the best mindset to have for the sake of her recovery, it's not the worst either.*
Sora: "Oh. Onii-chan?"
*When we arrive at the station, Sora tugs on my hand.*
Kakeru: "What's up?"
Sora: "I wanna stop by the convenience store."
Kakeru: "Sure, I don't mind."
Sora: "Wait here for me."
Kakeru: "Huh? Uh, okay."
Sora: "I'll be riiight back."
*Sora lets go of my hand and trots over to the convenience store.*
*She turns and waves to me as she pushes open the door, then hurries inside.*
*...*
*Now I'm starting to worry again.*
*Why'd she want to go in alone?*
*Why didn't she ask me if I wanted anything? Normally she would.*
*Besides, the karaoke place doesn't allow any outside food or drink. So what does she even want to buy?*
*None of these are significant enough on their own to warrant notice. Sora's so random I could probably just chalk this up to her acting on a whim, like she often does.*
*But right now, every little thing puts me on edge.*
*I'm definitely being overprotective.*
*Maybe Sora caught on to that, and that's part of why she wanted to go in alone.*
*I'm trying to act natural while still being cautious, but it's not going very well. Striking the right balance is hard.*
*Sora pops out of the store while I'm sighing at how pathetic I am.*
*She jogs over to me, a smile on her face.*
Sora: "I'm baaack!"
Kakeru: "You didn't buy anything?"
Sora: "Nope. Didn't see anything I wanted."
Kakeru: "Wanna hit up a different store, then?"
Sora: "Nah, I'm good. I don't feel like getting anything anymore."
Kakeru: "I see."
Sora: "Let's go!"
*She takes my hand and starts walking.*
*She doesn't feel like getting anything "anymore"?*
*So even though she didn't buy anything, she satisfied whatever desire she had?*
*...*
*You know what, I ought to just drop this. If I try to read into everything she says or does, I'll just spend the entire day brooding.*
*I should just enjoy myself. That's why we're having this outing in the first place.*
*And so we arrive at Round 2. This place has everything.*
*It's always packed after school, but at this hour the place is practically deserted.*
*It's still far from quiet, but the lack of customers gives the place a more relaxing feel than usual.*
Sora: "I always figured this place didn't open until like nine or ten, but I guess they open early."
Kakeru: "Man, we're real delinquents now, eh? Skipping school to go to Round 2."
Sora: "You said it! I bet Dad'll cry when he finds out."
Kakeru: "Just keep it a secret from him and you'll be fine."
Sora: "I'll guard the secret with my life. Hey, let's hit the arcade for a bit. There's still time until the movie starts."
Kakeru: "I thought you wanted to do karaoke first?"
Sora: "There's no time to sing. We've got a schedule to keep!"
Kakeru: "You've got a point there. Arcade it is."
Sora: "All right, then let's split up for a bit!"
Kakeru: "What?! But why?"
Sora: "There's a few games I wanna try on my own. Meet me back here in ten minutes."
*Sora dashes off to the arcade without waiting for my reply.*
*First the convenience store, now this. For how happy she was to be going out together, she sure wants an awful lot of alone time.*
*I guess in a way that's just like her, but...*
Kakeru: "..."
*I fold my arms and think.*
*If she wants to be left alone, I'm more than happy to do that.*
*But to be honest I'm not in the mood for arcade games, and while it's not like I have a particularly bad feeling about this or anything, I'm still a little worried about leaving Sora alone.*
*My instincts are warning me that I can't leave Sora's side, no matter for how short a time.*
*I make my decision and walk into the arcade as well.*
*Time to search for Sora.*
*If she's absorbed in a game, then that's fine. I'll just keep an eye on her.*
*If she complains about how I'm not playing anything, I'll just give her some excuse about how I don't want to waste my allowance, or how I have more fun watching than playing.*
*It doesn't take me long to find her.*
*She's playing the arcade version of one of those games where you control anthropomorphized battleships or planes or something. She's completely focused on the cabinet, manipulating the touch screen with a serious look on her face.*
*Now that I think about it, she sure is into a lot of different genres.*
*Intrigued, I walk over to get a closer look at the game.*
Kakeru: "Wha...?"
*But halfway there, I stop.*
*For a second, I completely forget what I was doing.*
*I squeeze my eyes shut and shake my head.*
Kakeru: "..."
*When I open my eyes again, Sora is standing there just like before.*
*...*
*I...*
*I lost sight of her.*
*I was looking at her the whole time. She was right there in front of me.*
*But there was a moment when I couldn't see Sora.*
Kakeru: "..."
*I'm shocked.*
*Nausea wells up within me, and I feel the blood draining from my face.*
*I reflexively cover my mouth and take a few steps back.*
*Sora's symptoms are worsening. She's not maintaining her current level of nonexistence.*
*The erosion of her Stigma is progressing.*
*I swore I'd never forget her, but...*
*...Just now, for the briefest of instants, I forgot about Sora.*
*A wave of dread washes over me.*
*I can't leave Sora alone. No, that's not strong enough.*
*I have to stay by her side at all times.*
Kakeru: "Sora!"
Sora: "Oh, what's up?"
*When I call out to her, Sora looks away from the screen and gets up from the arcade stool.*
Kakeru: "Oh, sorry, I didn't interrupt you, did I? Your game's still going, right?"
Sora: "Yeah, it's fine. Wasn't what I was hoping for."
Kakeru: "All right."
Sora: "'Sides, you sound like you were missing me."
Kakeru: "Like hell I was."
Sora: "Nah, I know you were. You've been staring at me the whole time with a lonely look on your face."
Kakeru: "You noticed? I mean, yeah, fine, I guess I was watching."
Sora: "If you're interested in the game, you can take over."
Kakeru: "Nah, I'm good. I don't care that much."
Sora: "Then c'mon, let's hit the movie theater!"
Kakeru: "Sure."
Sora: "Man, I can't believe I'm having so much fun on a school day. I'm feeling hella guilty right now."
Kakeru: "What movie are we gonna see?"
Sora: "Took you long enough to ask. Animaltopia."
Kakeru: "Oh, that's like, the CG one, right?"
Sora: "I mean, yeah, but man, when you put it that way you sound like our parents. Oh, and after lunch, we're gonna come back and see Lunaformers."
Kakeru: "Wow, going for a double feature? And seriously, we have to see that one? Didn't you see the reviews online?"
Sora: "God, it's supposed to be so rotten, but that's exactly why I wanna see it. Know what I mean?"
Kakeru: "I get where you're coming from, but can we at least get Lunaformers out of the way first if we're gonna watch both?"
Sora: "Nyope, this way works out best for the movie times. Come on, let's go."
*Sora takes my hand, and we head to the movie theater.*
*There's a smile on her face and a spring in her step.*
*I don't want to bring her down, so I put a lid on the fear bubbling up within me and try to act normal.*
*I can feel Sora's presence in the hand I'm holding.*
*She's right here.*
*Even if it starts to bother her, I won't let go. I'll hold onto this hand forever.*
*I swear it.*
*After watching both movies and singing our hearts out at karaoke, we head back home.*
*We really lived it up today.*
*Sora looks like she had a lot of fun.*
*I know I did.*
*However...*
*...*
*After spending the whole day with Sora, I picked up on something.*
*She tried to hide it, but I could tell.*
*I know I'm not just overthinking things now.*
*Sora's powers are out of control. Slowly but surely, they're eating away at her.*
*Her symptoms are worsening even faster than I expected.*
Kakeru: "Sora."
Sora: "Hmmm?"
*She replies casually, her eyes glued to the manga she's reading on my bed.*
*I spent ages figuring out how to broach the topic, but all my thoughts fly out the window the moment I say her name.*
*I end up baring my feelings.*
Kakeru: "...Do you just not trust me?"
Sora: "Huh?"
Kakeru: "Talk to me. I know you're hiding so much from me."
Sora: "..."
*Sora closes her manga and turns to face me.*
*She conjures up a stiff smile, but it crumbles instantly.*
*She can tell from my expression that there's no fooling me.*
*For a moment, she looks like she might cry.*
*But she pushes the tears back down.*
*And then she forces out another smile.*
Sora: "Can you get my phone for me?"
Kakeru: "Huh? Sure."
*I pick Sora's phone up off the table and hand it to her.*
*She pushes the power button to bring up the lock screen and shows it to me.*
Sora: "I'm not sure exactly what the story is, but... actual switches and buttons are no problem. If I push them, they work. But..."
*She taps the screen.*
*Types in her password.*
*However...*
*There's no change on the screen.*
Sora: "See? It doesn't do anything."
Kakeru: "Ah... Maybe it's just broken?"
*I know that's not the problem, but I can't stop myself from asking.*
*Sora just shakes her head.*
Sora: "That's what I was doing at the arcade. It was a touchscreen game, remember?"
Sora: "Looks like... machines can't tell I exist anymore. No matter what I tried in that game, nothing happened."
Sora: "..."
Sora: "People don't even notice I'm there now. Yesterday they at least realized I existed."
Sora: "My presence is basically zero all the time now. Even if I call out to someone, they don't react."
Kakeru: "That's why you went into the convenience store, isn't it?"
Sora: "Yep. I stood in front of the cash register and yelled 'excuse me' at the top of my lungs, and the cashier didn't say a word. They couldn't tell I was there."
Sora: "You noticed at some point, didn't you, onii-chan? It's like I'm not even here."
Kakeru: "Yeah. When we ordered lunch and the waiter put everything in front of me."
Sora: "Figures. That one's kinda hard to miss. Automatic doors don't even open for me unless you're there too, onii-chan."
Sora: "Onii-chan, I..."
Sora: "..."
Sora: "Sorry, never mind. I was just gonna complain, but... I won't."
Kakeru: "It's fine. Say whatever you want."
Sora: "I'm all right. I mean... Yeah, it's sad. Sure, Dad, Mom, all of my friends, Miya, Kosaka-senpai, Yuuki-senpai..."
Sora: "Everyone I know has forgotten all about me. Even machines ignore me. And that sucks, but..."
Sora: "But you're still here for me, onii-chan. As long as you're here, I'll be fine."
Sora: "Even if the rest of the world forgets my existence, as long as you don't, I'll still be happy."
Kakeru: "Ngh..."
Sora: "Come on, don't make that face. I'm not lying, really. As long as you're here for me, I'm okay. You're all I need."
Kakeru: "Just bear with it a while longer. As soon as that medicine's ready, we'll get you back to normal."
Sora: "...Mhm."
*Sora gives me a small nod.*
*And she whispers something under her breath.*
*I heard it loud and clear, though.*
*"But I might not last that long."*
*Pain lances through my chest, and my face twists in anguish.*
*Sora hurriedly puts on a smile.*
Sora: "Umm, I'm gonna go take a bath."
Kakeru: "Gotcha."
Sora: "Wanna come with?"
Kakeru: "Go ahead and take your time. By yourself."
Sora: "Aww, shucks."
*Sora gives me a look of mock disappointment before sliding off my bed. She grabs her towel and pajamas and heads to the bathroom.*
*...*
*She's vanishing from everyone's memories.*
*Not even machines register her existence anymore.*
*Could anything possibly be more isolating?*
*In a way, it's the same as dying.*
*But as long as I'm here, Sora can smile.*
*And yet...*
*I'm so worried.*
*I'm terrified I might not end up being able to keep my promise to her, and the thought sends chills down my spine.*
*Sora's out-of-control powers have even started taking their toll on me.*
*My symptoms are just in their earliest phase, so I should be fine for a while longer.*
*But if Sora realizes...*
*If she figures out that my memories of her are growing dimmer, and that I'm losing track of her presence at times...*
*Wait. What if she's already realized?*
*I might have started spacing out without even realizing it, and Sora would have picked up on that.*
*If I'm right about that, then maybe what Sora said is true.*
*She might not last long enough to be cured.*
*I have to do something.*
*I need to make sure that even if I end up forgetting, Sora can still be saved.*
Kakeru: "Sophie."
*I quietly call out to her.*
*Just like she promised the last time I saw her, Sophie appears immediately.*
*I hang my head and mumble softly, almost to myself...*
Kakeru: "We're out of time."
Sophitia: "I need only two more days."
Kakeru: "Two days?"
Sophitia: "Keep Sora hale until then. I'm working as fast as I can."
Kakeru: "...I have to ask you for something."
Sophitia: "Yes?"
Kakeru: "If I... end up forgetting about Sora, you have to give her the medicine in my place, Sophie."
Kakeru: "I'm starting to be affected by Sora's Artifact now too. But you have even more of that impedance or whatever than I do, right?"
Kakeru: "So please, I'm begging you. Promise you'll take care of Sora, if the worst happens to me."
Sophitia: "I'm afraid I can make no promises."
Kakeru: "Why?!"
Sophitia: "I am an observer. Not even Sora's powers can erase my memories. However, if Sora herself disappears, there's nothing I can do."
Kakeru: "If Sora...?"
Sophitia: "It will be as it was with Ghost. Her very existence will be erased from this world."
Sophitia: "If that were to happen, not even I could reverse it. Which is why I can't make any promises."
Kakeru: "...I see."
Kakeru: "..."
Kakeru: "I'm sorry. I shouldn't be losing my nerve like this. If I want to save Sora, I've gotta do better, that's all."
Sophitia: "You're not losing your nerve, just being careful. It's only natural to prepare for the worst-case scenario."
Sophitia: "However, you are quite out of sorts. Not only are you being pessimistic, you're also being too hard on yourself."
Sophitia: "Remember, Sora is the one who has it hardest of all. You're her brother. You need to be there for her."
Kakeru: "..."
Kakeru: "You're right. Thanks."
Sophitia: "To think I'd find myself delivering this sort of lecture. At any rate, it's all up to you. Keep Sora afloat for another two days."
Kakeru: "Got it. Leave it to me."
Sophitia: "That's the spirit."
*Sophie gives me a kind smile before disappearing through her portal.*
*Two more days. If I can stay strong for two more days, Sora will be saved.*
*How much longer until Sora reaches her absolute limit?*
*No, don't think about it. It doesn't matter.*
Sora: "Onii-chaaaaaaaaaaaaan! We got a disaster on our hands here! You're out of shampoo!"
Kakeru: "Oh, gimme one sec!"
*I get up and leave the living room.*
*I pull a spare bottle of shampoo from the cupboard underneath the sink and set it outside the bathroom door.*
Kakeru: "I'm leaving it out here."
Sora: "Thanksies!"
*That's Sora's voice.*
*She's still here. Still right here.*
*I just have to spend the next two days like I did this one.*
*That's all.*

*Even though we're both skipping school again, we wake up at the usual time in the morning.*
*But since there's no need to rush, we take our time with breakfast.*
*Halfway through my toast, I put my food down and pick up my phone. Now's a good time to call.*
*How many days in a row will this make? It's getting kind of hard to keep up the "I've got a cold" act, but it's too late to change my story now.*
Sora: "Calling Mom?"
Kakeru: "Yeah, gotta tell her to call in sick for me."
Sora: "She's still doing that 'are you a scammer' gag, isn't she?"
Kakeru: "God, she just will not stop. That joke died a year ago."
Sora: "Knowing Mom, she's gonna keep it up until even she doesn't think it's funny anymore."
Kakeru: "Last time I called her, I gave my name first to stop her, but then she got all sad over it. Guess I'll just have to play along."
*Grumbling, I press the call button.*
*After five or six rings, Mom finally picks up.*
Mom: "Hello?"
Kakeru: "Hey, it's me."
Mom: "Excuse me?"
Kakeru: "It's me, Kakeru."
Mom: "I'm sorry, but who are you?"
Kakeru: "Like I said, it's me, Kakeru. I know it's a drag, but would you mind—"
Mom: "Are you certain you haven't dialed the wrong number?"
Kakeru: "Seriously, are we still doing this? I didn't get the wrong number, and I'm not a scammer. It's your son, Kakeru."
Mom: "Huh?"
Kakeru: "Your son! I'm your son!"
Mom: "I don't have a son."
Kakeru: "Huh? Hey, w—"
Kakeru: "..."
*She hung up on me.*
*I thought she was just pushing the joke too far. I kept expecting her to laugh and say "How'd you like my acting this time?"*
*But... she wasn't.*
*She'd sounded legitimately annoyed. And moreover, she didn't remember me.*
*Don't tell me...*
*I'm starting to disappear as well?*
*Is Sora erasing me with her?*
Sora: "Onii-chan."
*Sora's voice snaps me out of my reverie.*
*She's looking intently at her right hand.*
Kakeru: "Hey... Sora...?"
Sora: "Onii-chan... What am I gonna do?"
*Tears are forming in her eyes.*
*Looking down, I see that her right hand has turned slightly translucent.*
*In fact, as the seconds tick away, I watch as the view of the room through her hand becomes ever more clear. She's vanishing before my eyes.*
*Even the toast pinched between her fingers is turning transparent.*
*Whether from sheer surprise or because her strength is fading, the toast slips from her fingers.*
*But even after she lets go of it, the toast remains translucent.*
*Sora's powers are going haywire.*
*Everything she touches, everything she wears, everything in her general vicinity is being erased.*
Sora: "What do I do? I...!"
Kakeru: "Sora!"
*I run to her side.*
*I clasp her fading hand between both of mine and squeeze tight.*
Sora: "I'm disappearing. I'm disappearing, onii-chan!"
Kakeru: "It's okay. You'll be all right. Just keep it together. You've gotta keep telling yourself that you're not going to let yourself disappear here!"
Sora: "But I—"
Kakeru: "Look at me, Sora! Look me in the eye!"
*She looks up at me, face full of hesitation and doubt.*
*Damn, now all of her is turning invisible!*
Kakeru: "Sora, can you see me?"
Sora: "Yeah."
Kakeru: "Can you feel my hands on yours right now?"
Sora: "Yeah. I can."
Kakeru: "Well, I can feel it too! I'm holding your hand, and I can feel it! I can feel your warmth in it!"
Kakeru: "Listen to me. You're still right here! You haven't disappeared! And you're not going anywhere!"
Sora: "But I..."
Kakeru: "Don't worry! I've got you! You won't disappear! I'm here with you. Your big brother's here with you!"
Sora: "Onii-chan. Onii-chan..."
*I take one hand away and throw it around her, pulling her close to me.*
*She grips my shirt with her other hand.*
*I lean into her ear and whisper over and over that she'll be okay.*
*I'm beyond desperate right now.*
*Fortunately, my desperation manages to serve as Sora's lifeline.*
Sora: "..."
*Little by little, Sora's breathing calms down.*
*Her fading outline solidifies once more.*
*Crisis averted. I think.*
*She's still here with me, for now.*
*What caused that sudden change? Why did her symptoms worsen so drastically just now?*
*I frantically cast about for an explanation.*
*However, regardless of what caused it, she's better now.*
*Nonetheless, Sora is clearly at her limit. We have even less time than I thought.*
*As long as I keep encouraging Sora like that, I want to believe that I can buy us more time.*
*I have to keep supporting her, for as long as it takes.*
*Meaning I have to keep smiling. I have to be the strong older brother she can rely on.*
Kakeru: "You did it, Sora."
*I gently stroke her hair.*
*Sora presses her forehead against mine and murmurs...*
Sora: "I'm sorry."

Kakeru: "I'm your brother. I don't need a reason to protect you. I'll always be here for you. So don't worry—you're not alone."
Sora: "Yeah."
*I pat her back.*
*Sora clings to me for a while longer.*
*After a few minutes, she finally rubs her eyes and breaks away.*
Sora: "Sorry. And thanks."
Kakeru: "...Come on, let's eat."
Sora: "Okay."
*Sora nods, and after a moment of hesitation, I return to my seat.*
*I feel like there's more I should have said to her.*
*"Are you all right?" "How are you feeling?" Either would be a stupid question. She's obviously not all right.*
*What more can I say?*
Sora: "..."
*Sora picks up the piece of toast she dropped, but instead of eating it she just puts it back on her plate.*
*The toast, too, has gone back to normal. It's no longer transparent.*
*Maybe Sora doesn't want to eat something she nearly made disappear.*
Kakeru: "Can I put another slice in the toaster for you?"
Sora: "No thanks. I'm fine."
Kakeru: "All right."
*It's getting harder and harder to think of something to say.*
*I watch Sora drink her glass of milk while I finish up my own piece of toast.*
*While I'm chewing, I think.*
*When it started, I was too panicked to think straight.*
*But now that I'm reflecting on it, there can be only one reason Sora's condition worsened.*
*She must have overheard my phone call. She knows what Mom said to me.*
*She's in shock because she figured out that her powers are affecting me as well.*
*And thus the abilities that she was just barely keeping in check suddenly went wild.*
*I was careless.*
*I can't help but beat myself up over my lack of foresight. If only I wasn't so damn stupid.*
*I should've realized. Up until yesterday, Kujo texted me after school every day to check on me. And then yesterday, she didn't.*
*I should have paid more attention to that anomaly, and then maybe I would have realized Sora's powers were affecting me sooner.*
*Or maybe I should have avoided calling Mom in front of Sora, just in case.*
*Still, it's no use crying over spilled milk.*
*I pop the last bit of toast into my mouth and crunch down on it hard, venting my frustrations on the hapless piece of bread before washing down the crumbs with a gulp of milk.*
Kakeru: "Are you sure you don't want more toast?"
Sora: "..."
*After a few seconds of silence, Sora nods.*
Kakeru: "All right."
*I stack up our plates and take them to the kitchen.*
*I put them in the sink and hurry back to the living room.*
*Sora is staring at the floor vacantly, not moving a muscle.*
*She was moments away from disappearing for good. I can't imagine how traumatizing an experience that must have been.*
*But she survived it. She's still hanging in there, even now.*
*I'm sure she can make it another day or two, somehow.*
Kakeru: "What do you say we go out again today? Are there any other movies you wanna see?"
Sora: "..."
Kakeru: "Ooh, or we could go back to the karaoke place? You did say you didn't get to sing as much as you wanted to."
Sora: "..."
Kakeru: "Yeah, you're right, it'd be exhausting to go out like that two days in a row. We can just relax at home, if you want."
Sora: "Onii-chan."
*Sora finally speaks.*
*But it's not to answer my question.*
*She picks up her phone, puts it down on the table, and pushes it toward me.*
Sora: "Can you unlock this for me?"
Kakeru: "Oh, sure."
*Nodding, I reach for her phone.*
*I know why she's asking.*
*I don't know Sora's password, but I doubt I'll need to ask.*
*I press the power button and tap the touch screen.*
*No matter where I touch, there's no response.*
*So. It's happening to me too now.*
*Funny. Just a few minutes ago I was fine.*
*That scare must have pushed Sora's powers to start affecting me more strongly.*
Sora: "I'm so sorry."
*She whispers.*
*She looks even more upset than she did when she was about to disappear.*
Sora: "I thought I'd be fine as long as I had you, onii-chan."
Sora: "That as long as you were with me, I didn't need anything else."
Sora: "..."
Sora: "But because of that, I ended up hurting you as well."
Kakeru: "Don't make it sound so negative. It's just like... I'm sharing the burden with you now!"
Kakeru: "Maybe that's what my Artifact's power is. Whatever I can do to help you, it makes me happy!"
Sora: "..."
*I try my best to encourage Sora.*
*But needless to say, she doesn't buy my pathetic excuse.*
*Sora hangs her head in despair.*
Sora: "Maybe it's better if I just disappear. Before I end up dragging you down with me."
Kakeru: "Sora!"
*I cry out.*
*And I lean forward and take her by the shoulders.*
*She watches me impassively.*
*When did the spark go out of her eyes?!*
Kakeru: "Never say that again!"
Sora: "But at this rate you'll disappear too, onii-chan."
Kakeru: "No I won't! I just told you, remember?! As long as you keep wishing to remain here, you won't disappear! You got over it not five minutes ago, remember?!"
Sora: "I'm not going to last much longer. I can tell, onii-chan."
Kakeru: "Don't say that! You're going to make it!"
Sora: "..."
*But no matter what I say, Sora remains listless.*
*It's like she's given up on everything. There's no emotion in her voice anymore.*
*Sophie's words echo in my mind.*
*She gave in. Her spirit is broken.*
*I swore I'd save her. I swore I wouldn't let this happen. But in spite of everything...*
*Sora's starting to wish for her own disappearance!*
*What do I do? What do I do? WHAT DO I DO?!*
Kakeru: "What can I do to make you feel better?"
Kakeru: "What do I have to do to make you smile again?"
Kakeru: "Please tell me. Tell me what to do! What do you want from me, Sora?!"
Kakeru: "If it'll ease your mind... If it'll make you smile, I'll... I'll do anything!"
Sora: "..."
Sora: "Anything?"

Kakeru: "But it's still a possibility. There's no telling how far she'll go. So long as she's alive, we won't be safe even in our homes."

Kakeru: "I don't want her to live in fear for another second."
